                    .offset(::core::mem::size_of::<mz_uint32>() as usize as isize)
                    .offset(2 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
                    << 16 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
                | (*(pZip64_end_of_central_dir
                    .offset(MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_SIZE_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                    as *const mz_uint8)
                    .offset(::core::mem::size_of::<mz_uint32>() as usize as isize)
                    .offset(3 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
                    << 24 as ::core::ffi::c_uint) as mz_uint64)
                << 32 as ::core::ffi::c_uint;
        if zip64_size_of_end_of_central_dir_record
            < (MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as ::core::ffi::c_int
                - 12 as ::core::ffi::c_int) as mz_uint64
        {
            return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_HEADER_OR_CORRUPTED);
        }
        if zip64_total_num_of_disks != 1 as mz_uint32 {
            return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_UNSUPPORTED_MULTIDISK);
        }
        if zip64_cdir_total_entries > MZ_UINT32_MAX as mz_uint64 {
            return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_TOO_MANY_FILES);
        }
        (*pZip).m_total_files = zip64_cdir_total_entries as mz_uint32;
        if zip64_cdir_total_entries_on_this_disk > MZ_UINT32_MAX as mz_uint64 {
            return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_TOO_MANY_FILES);
        }
        cdir_entries_on_this_disk = zip64_cdir_total_entries_on_this_disk as mz_uint32 as mz_uint;
        if zip64_size_of_central_directory > MZ_UINT32_MAX as mz_uint64 {
            return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_UNSUPPORTED_CDIR_SIZE);
        }

        num_this_disk = (*(pZip64_end_of_central_dir

                << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
            | (*(pZip64_end_of_central_dir
                .offset(MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_NUM_DISK_CDIR_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)

                << 16 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
            | (*(pZip64_end_of_central_dir

        cdir_ofs = (*(pZip64_end_of_central_dir
            .offset(MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_OFS_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
            as *const mz_uint8)
            .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32
            | (*(pZip64_end_of_central_dir
                .offset(MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_OFS_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                as *const mz_uint8)
                .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
                << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
            | (*(pZip64_end_of_central_dir
                .offset(MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_OFS_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                as *const mz_uint8)
                .offset(2 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
                << 16 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
            | (*(pZip64_end_of_central_dir
                .offset(MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_OFS_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                as *const mz_uint8)
                .offset(3 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
                << 24 as ::core::ffi::c_uint) as mz_uint64
            | ((*(pZip64_end_of_central_dir
                .offset(MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_OFS_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                as *const mz_uint8)
                .offset(::core::mem::size_of::<mz_uint32>() as usize as isize)
                .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32
                | (*(pZip64_end_of_central_dir
                    .offset(MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_OFS_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                    as *const mz_uint8)
                    .offset(::core::mem::size_of::<mz_uint32>() as usize as isize)
                    .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
                    << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
                | (*(pZip64_end_of_central_dir
                    .offset(MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_OFS_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                    as *const mz_uint8)
                    .offset(::core::mem::size_of::<mz_uint32>() as usize as isize)
                    .offset(2 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
                    << 16 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
                | (*(pZip64_end_of_central_dir
                    .offset(MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_OFS_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                    as *const mz_uint8)
                    .offset(::core::mem::size_of::<mz_uint32>() as usize as isize)
                    .offset(3 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
                    << 24 as ::core::ffi::c_uint) as mz_uint64)
                << 32 as ::core::ffi::c_uint;
    }
    if (*pZip).m_total_files != cdir_entries_on_this_disk {
        return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_UNSUPPORTED_MULTIDISK);
    }
    if num_this_disk | cdir_disk_index != 0 as mz_uint
        && (num_this_disk != 1 as mz_uint || cdir_disk_index != 1 as mz_uint)
    {
        return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_UNSUPPORTED_MULTIDISK);
    }
    if (cdir_size as mz_uint64)
        < ((*pZip).m_total_files as mz_uint64)
            .wrapping_mul(MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as ::core::ffi::c_int as mz_uint64)
    {
        return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_HEADER_OR_CORRUPTED);
    }
    if cdir_ofs.wrapping_add(cdir_size as mz_uint64) > (*pZip).m_archive_size {
        return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_HEADER_OR_CORRUPTED);
    }
    if eocd_ofs < cdir_ofs.wrapping_add(cdir_size as mz_uint64) {
        return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_HEADER_OR_CORRUPTED);
    }
    archive_ofs = eocd_ofs.wrapping_sub(cdir_ofs.wrapping_add(cdir_size as mz_uint64));
    if (*(*pZip).m_pState).m_zip64 != 0 {
        if archive_ofs
            < (MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as ::core::ffi::c_int
                + MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_LOCATOR_SIZE as ::core::ffi::c_int)
                as mz_uint64
        {
            return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_HEADER_OR_CORRUPTED);
        }
        archive_ofs = archive_ofs.wrapping_sub(
            (MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as ::core::ffi::c_int
                + MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_LOCATOR_SIZE as ::core::ffi::c_int)
                as mz_uint64,
        );
    }
    if ((*pZip).m_zip_type as ::core::ffi::c_uint
        == MZ_ZIP_TYPE_FILE as ::core::ffi::c_int as ::core::ffi::c_uint
        || (*pZip).m_zip_type as ::core::ffi::c_uint
            == MZ_ZIP_TYPE_CFILE as ::core::ffi::c_int as ::core::ffi::c_uint
        || (*pZip).m_zip_type as ::core::ffi::c_uint
            == MZ_ZIP_TYPE_USER as ::core::ffi::c_int as ::core::ffi::c_uint)
        && (*(*pZip).m_pState).m_file_archive_start_ofs == 0 as mz_uint64
    {
        (*(*pZip).m_pState).m_file_archive_start_ofs = archive_ofs;
        (*pZip).m_archive_size = (*pZip).m_archive_size.wrapping_sub(archive_ofs);
    }
    (*pZip).m_central_directory_file_ofs = cdir_ofs;
    if (*pZip).m_total_files != 0 {
        let mut i: mz_uint = 0;
        let mut n: mz_uint = 0;
        if mz_zip_array_resize(
            pZip,
            &raw mut (*(*pZip).m_pState).m_central_dir,
            cdir_size as size_t,
            MZ_FALSE as mz_uint,
        ) == 0
            || mz_zip_array_resize(
                pZip,
                &raw mut (*(*pZip).m_pState).m_central_dir_offsets,
                (*pZip).m_total_files as size_t,
                MZ_FALSE as mz_uint,
            ) == 0
        {
            return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_ALLOC_FAILED);
        }
        if sort_central_dir != 0 {
            if mz_zip_array_resize(
                pZip,
                &raw mut (*(*pZip).m_pState).m_sorted_central_dir_offsets,
                (*pZip).m_total_files as size_t,
                MZ_FALSE as mz_uint,
            ) == 0
            {
                return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_ALLOC_FAILED);
            }
        }
        if (*pZip).m_pRead.expect("non-null function pointer")(
            (*pZip).m_pIO_opaque,
            cdir_ofs,
            (*(*pZip).m_pState).m_central_dir.m_p,
            cdir_size as size_t,
        ) != cdir_size as size_t
        {
            return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_FILE_READ_FAILED);
        }
        p = (*(*pZip).m_pState).m_central_dir.m_p as *const mz_uint8;
        n = cdir_size;
        i = 0 as mz_uint;
        while i < (*pZip).m_total_files {
            let mut total_header_size: mz_uint = 0;
            let mut disk_index: mz_uint = 0;
            let mut bit_flags: mz_uint = 0;
            let mut filename_size: mz_uint = 0;
            let mut ext_data_size: mz_uint = 0;
            let mut comp_size: mz_uint64 = 0;
            let mut decomp_size: mz_uint64 = 0;
            let mut local_header_ofs: mz_uint64 = 0;
            if n < MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as ::core::ffi::c_int as mz_uint
                || *p.offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32
                    | (*p.offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
                        << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
                    | (*p.offset(2 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
                        << 16 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
                    | (*p.offset(3 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
                        << 24 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
                    != MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG as ::core::ffi::c_int as mz_uint32
            {
                return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_HEADER_OR_CORRUPTED);
            }
            *((*(*pZip).m_pState).m_central_dir_offsets.m_p as *mut mz_uint32).offset(i as isize) =
                p.offset_from((*(*pZip).m_pState).m_central_dir.m_p as *const mz_uint8)
                    as ::core::ffi::c_long as mz_uint32;
            if sort_central_dir != 0 {
                *((*(*pZip).m_pState).m_sorted_central_dir_offsets.m_p as *mut mz_uint32)
                    .offset(i as isize) = i as mz_uint32;
            }
            comp_size = (*p
                .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32
                | (*p
                    .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                    .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
                    << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
                | (*p
                    .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                    .offset(2 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
                    << 16 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
                | (*p
                    .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                    .offset(3 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
                    << 24 as ::core::ffi::c_uint) as mz_uint64;
            decomp_size = (*p
                .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_DECOMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32
                | (*p
                    .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_DECOMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                    .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
                    << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
                | (*p
                    .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_DECOMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                    .offset(2 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
                    << 16 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
                | (*p
                    .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_DECOMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                    .offset(3 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
                    << 24 as ::core::ffi::c_uint) as mz_uint64;
            local_header_ofs = (*p
                .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_LOCAL_HEADER_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                as mz_uint32
                | (*p
                    .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_LOCAL_HEADER_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                    .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
                    << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
                | (*p
                    .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_LOCAL_HEADER_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                    .offset(2 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
                    << 16 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
                | (*p
                    .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_LOCAL_HEADER_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                    .offset(3 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
                    << 24 as ::core::ffi::c_uint) as mz_uint64;
            filename_size = (*p
                .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32
                | (*p
                    .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                    .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
                    << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint) as mz_uint;
            ext_data_size = (*p
                .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32
                | (*p
                    .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                    .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
                    << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint) as mz_uint;
            if (*(*pZip).m_pState).m_zip64_has_extended_info_fields == 0
                && ext_data_size != 0
                && (if (if comp_size > decomp_size {
                    comp_size
                } else {
                    decomp_size
                }) > local_header_ofs
                {
                    (if comp_size > decomp_size {
                        comp_size
                    } else {
                        decomp_size
                    })
                } else {
                    local_header_ofs
                }) == MZ_UINT32_MAX as mz_uint64
            {
                let mut extra_size_remaining: mz_uint32 = ext_data_size as mz_uint32;
                if extra_size_remaining != 0 {
                    let mut pExtra_data: *const mz_uint8 = ::core::ptr::null::<mz_uint8>();
                    let mut buf: *mut ::core::ffi::c_void = NULL;
                    if (MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as ::core::ffi::c_int as mz_uint)
                        .wrapping_add(filename_size)
                        .wrapping_add(ext_data_size)
                        > n
                    {
                        buf = malloc(ext_data_size as size_t);
                        if buf.is_null() {
                            return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_ALLOC_FAILED);
                        }
                        if (*pZip).m_pRead.expect("non-null function pointer")(
                            (*pZip).m_pIO_opaque,
                            cdir_ofs
                                .wrapping_add(
                                    MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as ::core::ffi::c_int
                                        as mz_uint64,
                                )
                                .wrapping_add(filename_size as mz_uint64),
                            buf,
                            ext_data_size as size_t,
                        ) != ext_data_size as size_t
                        {
                            free(buf);
                            return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_FILE_READ_FAILED);
                        }
                        pExtra_data = buf as *mut mz_uint8;
                    } else {
                        pExtra_data = p
                            .offset(MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                            .offset(filename_size as isize);
                    }
                    loop {
                        let mut field_id: mz_uint32 = 0;
                        let mut field_data_size: mz_uint32 = 0;
                        if (extra_size_remaining as usize)
                            < (::core::mem::size_of::<mz_uint16>() as usize)
                                .wrapping_mul(2 as usize)
                        {
                            free(buf);
                            return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_HEADER_OR_CORRUPTED);
                        }
                        field_id = *pExtra_data.offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                            as mz_uint32
                            | (*pExtra_data.offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
                                << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint;
                        field_data_size = *pExtra_data
                            .offset(::core::mem::size_of::<mz_uint16>() as usize as isize)
                            .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                            as mz_uint32
                            | (*pExtra_data
                                .offset(::core::mem::size_of::<mz_uint16>() as usize as isize)
                                .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                                as mz_uint32)
                                << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint;
                        if (field_data_size as usize).wrapping_add(
                            (::core::mem::size_of::<mz_uint16>() as usize).wrapping_mul(2 as usize),
                        ) > extra_size_remaining as usize
                        {
                            free(buf);
                            return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_HEADER_OR_CORRUPTED);
                        }
                        if field_id
                            == MZ_ZIP64_EXTENDED_INFORMATION_FIELD_HEADER_ID as ::core::ffi::c_int
                                as mz_uint32
                        {
                            (*(*pZip).m_pState).m_zip64 = MZ_TRUE as mz_bool;
                            (*(*pZip).m_pState).m_zip64_has_extended_info_fields =
                                MZ_TRUE as mz_bool;
                            break;
                        } else {
                            pExtra_data = pExtra_data.offset(
                                (::core::mem::size_of::<mz_uint16>() as usize)
                                    .wrapping_mul(2 as usize)
                                    .wrapping_add(field_data_size as usize)
                                    as isize,
                            );
                            extra_size_remaining = (extra_size_remaining as usize)
                                .wrapping_sub(
                                    (::core::mem::size_of::<mz_uint16>() as usize)
                                        .wrapping_mul(2 as usize),
                                )
                                .wrapping_sub(field_data_size as usize)
                                as mz_uint32;
                            if !(extra_size_remaining != 0) {
                                break;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    free(buf);
                }
            }
            if comp_size != MZ_UINT32_MAX as mz_uint64 && decomp_size != MZ_UINT32_MAX as mz_uint64
            {
                if *p
                    .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_METHOD_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                    .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32
                    | (*p
                        .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_METHOD_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                        .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                        as mz_uint32)
                        << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
                    | (*p
                        .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_METHOD_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                        .offset(2 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                        as mz_uint32)
                        << 16 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
                    | (*p
                        .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_METHOD_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                        .offset(3 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                        as mz_uint32)
                        << 24 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
                    == 0
                    && decomp_size != comp_size
                    || decomp_size != 0 && comp_size == 0
                {
                    return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_HEADER_OR_CORRUPTED);
                }
            }
            disk_index = (*p
                .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_DISK_START_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32
                | (*p
                    .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_DISK_START_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                    .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
                    << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint) as mz_uint;
            if disk_index == MZ_UINT16_MAX as mz_uint
                || disk_index != num_this_disk && disk_index != 1 as mz_uint
            {
                return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_UNSUPPORTED_MULTIDISK);
            }
            if comp_size != MZ_UINT32_MAX as mz_uint64 {
                if ((*p
                    .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_LOCAL_HEADER_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                    .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32
                    | (*p
                        .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_LOCAL_HEADER_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                        .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                        as mz_uint32)
                        << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
                    | (*p
                        .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_LOCAL_HEADER_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                        .offset(2 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                        as mz_uint32)
                        << 16 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
                    | (*p
                        .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_LOCAL_HEADER_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                        .offset(3 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                        as mz_uint32)
                        << 24 as ::core::ffi::c_uint) as mz_uint64)
                    .wrapping_add(MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as ::core::ffi::c_int as mz_uint64)
                    .wrapping_add(comp_size)
                    > (*pZip).m_archive_size
                {
                    return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_HEADER_OR_CORRUPTED);
                }
            }
            bit_flags = (*p
                .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_BIT_FLAG_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32
                | (*p
                    .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_BIT_FLAG_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                    .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
                    << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint) as mz_uint;
            if bit_flags
                & MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_LOCAL_DIR_IS_MASKED as ::core::ffi::c_int
                    as mz_uint
                != 0
            {
                return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_UNSUPPORTED_ENCRYPTION);
            }
            total_header_size = (MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as ::core::ffi::c_int as mz_uint32)
                .wrapping_add(
                    *p.offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                        .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32
                        | (*p
                            .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                            .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                            as mz_uint32)
                            << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint,
                )
                .wrapping_add(
                    *p.offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                        .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32
                        | (*p
                            .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                            .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                            as mz_uint32)
                            << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint,
                )
                .wrapping_add(
                    *p.offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMMENT_LEN_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                        .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32
                        | (*p
                            .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMMENT_LEN_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                            .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                            as mz_uint32)
                            << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint,
                ) as mz_uint;
            if total_header_size > n {
                return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_HEADER_OR_CORRUPTED);
            }
            n = n.wrapping_sub(total_header_size);
            p = p.offset(total_header_size as isize);
            i = i.wrapping_add(1);
        }
    }
    if sort_central_dir != 0 {
        mz_zip_reader_sort_central_dir_offsets_by_filename(pZip);
    }
    return MZ_TRUE;
}
#[no_mangle]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn mz_zip_zero_struct(mut pZip: *mut mz_zip_archive) {
    if !pZip.is_null() {
        memset(
            pZip as *mut ::core::ffi::c_void,
            0 as ::core::ffi::c_int,
            ::core::mem::size_of::<mz_zip_archive>() as size_t,
        );
    }
}
unsafe extern "C" fn mz_zip_reader_end_internal(
    mut pZip: *mut mz_zip_archive,
    mut set_last_error: mz_bool,
) -> mz_bool {
    let mut status: mz_bool = MZ_TRUE;
    if pZip.is_null() {
        return MZ_FALSE;
    }
    if (*pZip).m_pState.is_null()
        || (*pZip).m_pAlloc.is_none()
        || (*pZip).m_pFree.is_none()
        || (*pZip).m_zip_mode as ::core::ffi::c_uint
            != MZ_ZIP_MODE_READING as ::core::ffi::c_int as ::core::ffi::c_uint
    {
        if set_last_error != 0 {
            (*pZip).m_last_error = MZ_ZIP_INVALID_PARAMETER;
        }
        return MZ_FALSE;
    }
    if !(*pZip).m_pState.is_null() {
        let mut pState: *mut mz_zip_internal_state = (*pZip).m_pState;
        (*pZip).m_pState = ::core::ptr::null_mut::<mz_zip_internal_state>();
        mz_zip_array_clear(pZip, &raw mut (*pState).m_central_dir);
        mz_zip_array_clear(pZip, &raw mut (*pState).m_central_dir_offsets);
        mz_zip_array_clear(pZip, &raw mut (*pState).m_sorted_central_dir_offsets);
        if !(*pState).m_pFile.is_null() {
            if (*pZip).m_zip_type as ::core::ffi::c_uint
                == MZ_ZIP_TYPE_FILE as ::core::ffi::c_int as ::core::ffi::c_uint
            {
                if fclose((*pState).m_pFile) == EOF {
                    if set_last_error != 0 {
                        (*pZip).m_last_error = MZ_ZIP_FILE_CLOSE_FAILED;
                    }
                    status = MZ_FALSE as mz_bool;
                }
            }
            (*pState).m_pFile = ::core::ptr::null_mut::<FILE>();
        }
        (*pZip).m_pFree.expect("non-null function pointer")(
            (*pZip).m_pAlloc_opaque,
            pState as *mut ::core::ffi::c_void,
        );
    }
    (*pZip).m_zip_mode = MZ_ZIP_MODE_INVALID;
    return status;
}
#[no_mangle]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn mz_zip_reader_end(mut pZip: *mut mz_zip_archive) -> mz_bool {
    return mz_zip_reader_end_internal(pZip, MZ_TRUE);
}
#[no_mangle]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn mz_zip_reader_init(
    mut pZip: *mut mz_zip_archive,
    mut size: mz_uint64,
    mut flags: mz_uint,
) -> mz_bool {
    if pZip.is_null() || (*pZip).m_pRead.is_none() {
        return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_PARAMETER);
    }
    if mz_zip_reader_init_internal(pZip, flags) == 0 {
        return MZ_FALSE;
    }
    (*pZip).m_zip_type = MZ_ZIP_TYPE_USER;
    (*pZip).m_archive_size = size;
    if mz_zip_reader_read_central_dir(pZip, flags) == 0 {
        mz_zip_reader_end_internal(pZip, MZ_FALSE);
        return MZ_FALSE;
    }
    return MZ_TRUE;
}
unsafe extern "C" fn mz_zip_mem_read_func(
    mut pOpaque: *mut ::core::ffi::c_void,
    mut file_ofs: mz_uint64,
    mut pBuf: *mut ::core::ffi::c_void,
    mut n: size_t,
) -> size_t {
    let mut pZip: *mut mz_zip_archive = pOpaque as *mut mz_zip_archive;
    let mut s: size_t = if file_ofs >= (*pZip).m_archive_size {
        0 as size_t
    } else {
        (if (*pZip).m_archive_size.wrapping_sub(file_ofs) < n as mz_uint64 {
            (*pZip).m_archive_size.wrapping_sub(file_ofs)
        } else {
            n as mz_uint64
        }) as size_t
    };
    memcpy(
        pBuf,
        ((*(*pZip).m_pState).m_pMem as *const mz_uint8).offset(file_ofs as isize)
            as *const ::core::ffi::c_void,
        s,
    );
    return s;
}
#[no_mangle]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn mz_zip_reader_init_mem(
    mut pZip: *mut mz_zip_archive,
    mut pMem: *const ::core::ffi::c_void,
    mut size: size_t,
    mut flags: mz_uint,
) -> mz_bool {
    if pMem.is_null() {
        return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_PARAMETER);
    }
    if size < MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as ::core::ffi::c_int as size_t {
        return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_NOT_AN_ARCHIVE);
    }
    if mz_zip_reader_init_internal(pZip, flags) == 0 {
        return MZ_FALSE;
    }
    (*pZip).m_zip_type = MZ_ZIP_TYPE_MEMORY;
    (*pZip).m_archive_size = size as mz_uint64;
    (*pZip).m_pRead = Some(
        mz_zip_mem_read_func
            as unsafe extern "C" fn(
                *mut ::core::ffi::c_void,
                mz_uint64,
                *mut ::core::ffi::c_void,
                size_t,
            ) -> size_t,
    ) as mz_file_read_func;
    (*pZip).m_pIO_opaque = pZip as *mut ::core::ffi::c_void;
    (*pZip).m_pNeeds_keepalive = None;
    (*(*pZip).m_pState).m_pMem = pMem as *mut ::core::ffi::c_void;
    (*(*pZip).m_pState).m_mem_size = size;
    if mz_zip_reader_read_central_dir(pZip, flags) == 0 {
        mz_zip_reader_end_internal(pZip, MZ_FALSE);
        return MZ_FALSE;
    }
    return MZ_TRUE;
}
unsafe extern "C" fn mz_zip_file_read_func(
    mut pOpaque: *mut ::core::ffi::c_void,
    mut file_ofs: mz_uint64,
    mut pBuf: *mut ::core::ffi::c_void,
    mut n: size_t,
) -> size_t {
    let mut pZip: *mut mz_zip_archive = pOpaque as *mut mz_zip_archive;
    let mut cur_ofs: mz_int64 = ftello((*(*pZip).m_pState).m_pFile) as mz_int64;
    file_ofs = file_ofs.wrapping_add((*(*pZip).m_pState).m_file_archive_start_ofs);
    if (file_ofs as mz_int64) < 0 as mz_int64
        || cur_ofs != file_ofs as mz_int64
            && fseeko((*(*pZip).m_pState).m_pFile, file_ofs as __off_t, SEEK_SET) != 0
    {
        return 0 as size_t;
    }
    return fread(pBuf, 1 as size_t, n, (*(*pZip).m_pState).m_pFile) as size_t;
}
#[no_mangle]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn mz_zip_reader_init_file(
    mut pZip: *mut mz_zip_archive,
    mut pFilename: *const ::core::ffi::c_char,
    mut flags: mz_uint32,
) -> mz_bool {
    return mz_zip_reader_init_file_v2(
        pZip,
        pFilename,
        flags as mz_uint,
        0 as mz_uint64,
        0 as mz_uint64,
    );
}
#[no_mangle]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn mz_zip_reader_init_file_v2(
    mut pZip: *mut mz_zip_archive,
    mut pFilename: *const ::core::ffi::c_char,
    mut flags: mz_uint,
    mut file_start_ofs: mz_uint64,
    mut archive_size: mz_uint64,
) -> mz_bool {
    let mut file_size: mz_uint64 = 0;
    let mut pFile: *mut FILE = ::core::ptr::null_mut::<FILE>();
    if pZip.is_null()
        || pFilename.is_null()
        || archive_size != 0
            && archive_size
                < MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as ::core::ffi::c_int as mz_uint64
    {
        return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_PARAMETER);
    }
    pFile = fopen(
        pFilename,
        if flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_READ_ALLOW_WRITING as ::core::ffi::c_int as mz_uint != 0 {
            b"r+b\0" as *const u8 as *const ::core::ffi::c_char
        } else {
            b"rb\0" as *const u8 as *const ::core::ffi::c_char
        },
    ) as *mut FILE;
    if pFile.is_null() {
        return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_FILE_OPEN_FAILED);
    }
    file_size = archive_size;
    if file_size == 0 {
        if fseeko(pFile, 0 as __off_t, SEEK_END) != 0 {
            fclose(pFile);
            return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_FILE_SEEK_FAILED);
        }
        file_size = ftello(pFile) as mz_uint64;
    }
    if file_size < MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as ::core::ffi::c_int as mz_uint64 {
        fclose(pFile);
        return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_NOT_AN_ARCHIVE);
    }
    if mz_zip_reader_init_internal(pZip, flags) == 0 {
        fclose(pFile);
        return MZ_FALSE;
    }
    (*pZip).m_zip_type = MZ_ZIP_TYPE_FILE;
    (*pZip).m_pRead = Some(
        mz_zip_file_read_func
            as unsafe extern "C" fn(
                *mut ::core::ffi::c_void,
                mz_uint64,
                *mut ::core::ffi::c_void,
                size_t,
            ) -> size_t,
    ) as mz_file_read_func;
    (*pZip).m_pIO_opaque = pZip as *mut ::core::ffi::c_void;
    (*(*pZip).m_pState).m_pFile = pFile;
    (*pZip).m_archive_size = file_size;
    (*(*pZip).m_pState).m_file_archive_start_ofs = file_start_ofs;
    if mz_zip_reader_read_central_dir(pZip, flags) == 0 {
        mz_zip_reader_end_internal(pZip, MZ_FALSE);
        return MZ_FALSE;
    }
    return MZ_TRUE;
}
#[no_mangle]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn mz_zip_reader_init_cfile(
    mut pZip: *mut mz_zip_archive,
    mut pFile: *mut FILE,
    mut archive_size: mz_uint64,
    mut flags: mz_uint,
) -> mz_bool {
    let mut cur_file_ofs: mz_uint64 = 0;
    if pZip.is_null() || pFile.is_null() {
        return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_FILE_OPEN_FAILED);
    }
    cur_file_ofs = ftello(pFile) as mz_uint64;
    if archive_size == 0 {
        if fseeko(pFile, 0 as __off_t, SEEK_END) != 0 {
            return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_FILE_SEEK_FAILED);
        }
        archive_size = (ftello(pFile) as mz_uint64).wrapping_sub(cur_file_ofs);
        if archive_size < MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as ::core::ffi::c_int as mz_uint64 {
            return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_NOT_AN_ARCHIVE);
        }
    }
    if mz_zip_reader_init_internal(pZip, flags) == 0 {
        return MZ_FALSE;
    }
    (*pZip).m_zip_type = MZ_ZIP_TYPE_CFILE;
    (*pZip).m_pRead = Some(
        mz_zip_file_read_func
            as unsafe extern "C" fn(
                *mut ::core::ffi::c_void,
                mz_uint64,
                *mut ::core::ffi::c_void,
                size_t,
            ) -> size_t,
    ) as mz_file_read_func;
    (*pZip).m_pIO_opaque = pZip as *mut ::core::ffi::c_void;
    (*(*pZip).m_pState).m_pFile = pFile;
    (*pZip).m_archive_size = archive_size;
    (*(*pZip).m_pState).m_file_archive_start_ofs = cur_file_ofs;
    if mz_zip_reader_read_central_dir(pZip, flags) == 0 {
        mz_zip_reader_end_internal(pZip, MZ_FALSE);
        return MZ_FALSE;
    }
    return MZ_TRUE;
}
#[inline(always)]
unsafe extern "C" fn mz_zip_get_cdh(
    mut pZip: *mut mz_zip_archive,
    mut file_index: mz_uint,
) -> *const mz_uint8 {
    if pZip.is_null() || (*pZip).m_pState.is_null() || file_index >= (*pZip).m_total_files {
        return ::core::ptr::null::<mz_uint8>();
    }
    return ((*(*pZip).m_pState).m_central_dir.m_p as *mut mz_uint8).offset(
        *((*(*pZip).m_pState).m_central_dir_offsets.m_p as *mut mz_uint32)
            .offset(file_index as isize) as isize,
    ) as *mut mz_uint8;
}
#[no_mangle]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn mz_zip_reader_is_file_encrypted(
    mut pZip: *mut mz_zip_archive,
    mut file_index: mz_uint,
) -> mz_bool {
    let mut m_bit_flag: mz_uint = 0;
    let mut p: *const mz_uint8 = mz_zip_get_cdh(pZip, file_index);
    if p.is_null() {
        mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_PARAMETER);
        return MZ_FALSE;
    }
    m_bit_flag = (*p
        .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_BIT_FLAG_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
        .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32
        | (*p
            .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_BIT_FLAG_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
            .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
            << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint) as mz_uint;
    return (m_bit_flag
        & (MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_IS_ENCRYPTED as ::core::ffi::c_int
            | MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_USES_STRONG_ENCRYPTION as ::core::ffi::c_int)
            as mz_uint
        != 0 as mz_uint) as ::core::ffi::c_int;
}
#[no_mangle]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn mz_zip_reader_is_file_supported(
    mut pZip: *mut mz_zip_archive,
    mut file_index: mz_uint,
) -> mz_bool {
    let mut bit_flag: mz_uint = 0;
    let mut method: mz_uint = 0;
    let mut p: *const mz_uint8 = mz_zip_get_cdh(pZip, file_index);
    if p.is_null() {
        mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_PARAMETER);
        return MZ_FALSE;
    }
    method = (*p
        .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_METHOD_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
        .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32
        | (*p
            .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_METHOD_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
            .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
            << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint) as mz_uint;
    bit_flag = (*p
        .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_BIT_FLAG_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
        .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32
        | (*p
            .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_BIT_FLAG_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
            .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
            << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint) as mz_uint;
    if method != 0 as mz_uint && method != MZ_DEFLATED as mz_uint {
        mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_UNSUPPORTED_METHOD);
        return MZ_FALSE;
    }
    if bit_flag
        & (MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_IS_ENCRYPTED as ::core::ffi::c_int
            | MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_USES_STRONG_ENCRYPTION as ::core::ffi::c_int)
            as mz_uint
        != 0
    {
        mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_UNSUPPORTED_ENCRYPTION);
        return MZ_FALSE;
    }
    if bit_flag
        & MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_COMPRESSED_PATCH_FLAG as ::core::ffi::c_int as mz_uint
        != 0
    {
        mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_UNSUPPORTED_FEATURE);
        return MZ_FALSE;
    }
    return MZ_TRUE;
}
#[no_mangle]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn mz_zip_reader_is_file_a_directory(
    mut pZip: *mut mz_zip_archive,
    mut file_index: mz_uint,
) -> mz_bool {
    let mut filename_len: mz_uint = 0;
    let mut attribute_mapping_id: mz_uint = 0;
    let mut external_attr: mz_uint = 0;
    let mut p: *const mz_uint8 = mz_zip_get_cdh(pZip, file_index);
    if p.is_null() {
        mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_PARAMETER);
        return MZ_FALSE;
    }
    filename_len = (*p
        .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
        .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32
        | (*p
            .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
            .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
            << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint) as mz_uint;
    if filename_len != 0 {
        if *p
            .offset(MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
            .offset(filename_len as isize)
            .offset(-(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)) as ::core::ffi::c_int
            == '/' as i32
        {
            return MZ_TRUE;
        }
    }
    attribute_mapping_id = ((*p
        .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_VERSION_MADE_BY_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
        .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32
        | (*p
            .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_VERSION_MADE_BY_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
            .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
            << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint)
        >> 8 as ::core::ffi::c_int) as mz_uint;
    external_attr = (*p
        .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTERNAL_ATTR_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
        .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32
        | (*p
            .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTERNAL_ATTR_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
            .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
            << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
        | (*p
            .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTERNAL_ATTR_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
            .offset(2 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
            << 16 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
        | (*p
            .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTERNAL_ATTR_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
            .offset(3 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
            << 24 as ::core::ffi::c_uint) as mz_uint;
    if external_attr & MZ_ZIP_DOS_DIR_ATTRIBUTE_BITFLAG as ::core::ffi::c_int as mz_uint
        != 0 as mz_uint
    {
        return MZ_TRUE;
    }
    return MZ_FALSE;
}
unsafe extern "C" fn mz_zip_file_stat_internal(
    mut pZip: *mut mz_zip_archive,
    mut file_index: mz_uint,
    mut pCentral_dir_header: *const mz_uint8,
    mut pStat: *mut mz_zip_archive_file_stat,
    mut pFound_zip64_extra_data: *mut mz_bool,
) -> mz_bool {
    let mut n: mz_uint = 0;
    let mut p: *const mz_uint8 = pCentral_dir_header;
    if !pFound_zip64_extra_data.is_null() {
        *pFound_zip64_extra_data = MZ_FALSE as mz_bool;
    }
    if p.is_null() || pStat.is_null() {
        return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_PARAMETER);
    }
    (*pStat).m_file_index = file_index as mz_uint32;
    (*pStat).m_central_dir_ofs = *((*(*pZip).m_pState).m_central_dir_offsets.m_p as *mut mz_uint32)
        .offset(file_index as isize) as mz_uint64;
    (*pStat).m_version_made_by = (*p
        .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_VERSION_MADE_BY_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
        .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32
        | (*p
            .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_VERSION_MADE_BY_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
            .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
            << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint) as mz_uint16;
    (*pStat).m_version_needed = (*p
        .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_VERSION_NEEDED_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
        .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32
        | (*p
            .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_VERSION_NEEDED_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
            .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
            << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint) as mz_uint16;
    (*pStat).m_bit_flag = (*p
        .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_BIT_FLAG_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
        .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32
        | (*p
            .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_BIT_FLAG_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
            .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
            << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint) as mz_uint16;
    (*pStat).m_method = (*p
        .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_METHOD_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
        .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32
        | (*p
            .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_METHOD_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
            .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
            << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint) as mz_uint16;
    (*pStat).m_time = mz_zip_dos_to_time_t(
        (*p.offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILE_TIME_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
            .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32
            | (*p
                .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILE_TIME_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
                << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint) as ::core::ffi::c_int,
        (*p.offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILE_DATE_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
            .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32
            | (*p
                .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILE_DATE_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
                << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint) as ::core::ffi::c_int,
    );
    (*pStat).m_crc32 = *p
        .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_CRC32_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
        .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32
        | (*p
            .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_CRC32_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
            .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
            << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
        | (*p
            .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_CRC32_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
            .offset(2 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
            << 16 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
        | (*p
            .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_CRC32_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
            .offset(3 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
            << 24 as ::core::ffi::c_uint;
    (*pStat).m_comp_size = (*p
        .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
        .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32
        | (*p
            .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
            .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
            << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
        | (*p
            .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
            .offset(2 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
            << 16 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
        | (*p
            .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
            .offset(3 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
            << 24 as ::core::ffi::c_uint) as mz_uint64;
    (*pStat).m_uncomp_size = (*p
        .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_DECOMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
        .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32
        | (*p
            .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_DECOMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
            .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
            << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
        | (*p
            .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_DECOMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
            .offset(2 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
            << 16 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
        | (*p
            .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_DECOMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
            .offset(3 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
            << 24 as ::core::ffi::c_uint) as mz_uint64;
    (*pStat).m_internal_attr = (*p
        .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_INTERNAL_ATTR_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
        .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32
        | (*p
            .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_INTERNAL_ATTR_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
            .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
            << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint) as mz_uint16;
    (*pStat).m_external_attr = *p
        .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTERNAL_ATTR_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
        .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32
        | (*p
            .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTERNAL_ATTR_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
            .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
            << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
        | (*p
            .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTERNAL_ATTR_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
            .offset(2 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
            << 16 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
        | (*p
            .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTERNAL_ATTR_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
            .offset(3 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
            << 24 as ::core::ffi::c_uint;
    (*pStat).m_local_header_ofs = (*p
        .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_LOCAL_HEADER_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
        .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32
        | (*p
            .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_LOCAL_HEADER_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
            .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
            << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
        | (*p
            .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_LOCAL_HEADER_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
            .offset(2 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
            << 16 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
        | (*p
            .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_LOCAL_HEADER_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
            .offset(3 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
            << 24 as ::core::ffi::c_uint) as mz_uint64;
    n = (*p
        .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
        .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32
        | (*p
            .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
            .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
            << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint) as mz_uint;
    n = if n
        < (MZ_ZIP_MAX_ARCHIVE_FILENAME_SIZE as ::core::ffi::c_int - 1 as ::core::ffi::c_int)
            as mz_uint
    {
        n
    } else {
        (MZ_ZIP_MAX_ARCHIVE_FILENAME_SIZE as ::core::ffi::c_int - 1 as ::core::ffi::c_int)
            as mz_uint
    };
    memcpy(
        &raw mut (*pStat).m_filename as *mut ::core::ffi::c_char as *mut ::core::ffi::c_void,
        p.offset(MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
            as *const ::core::ffi::c_void,
        n as size_t,
    );
    (*pStat).m_filename[n as usize] = '\0' as i32 as ::core::ffi::c_char;
    n = (*p
        .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMMENT_LEN_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
        .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32
        | (*p
            .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMMENT_LEN_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
            .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
            << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint) as mz_uint;
    n = if n
        < (MZ_ZIP_MAX_ARCHIVE_FILE_COMMENT_SIZE as ::core::ffi::c_int - 1 as ::core::ffi::c_int)
            as mz_uint
    {
        n
    } else {
        (MZ_ZIP_MAX_ARCHIVE_FILE_COMMENT_SIZE as ::core::ffi::c_int - 1 as ::core::ffi::c_int)
            as mz_uint
    };
    (*pStat).m_comment_size = n as mz_uint32;
    memcpy(
        &raw mut (*pStat).m_comment as *mut ::core::ffi::c_char as *mut ::core::ffi::c_void,
        p.offset(MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
            .offset(
                (*p.offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                    .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32
                    | (*p
                        .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                        .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                        as mz_uint32)
                        << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint) as isize,
            )
            .offset(
                (*p.offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                    .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32
                    | (*p
                        .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                        .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                        as mz_uint32)
                        << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint) as isize,
            ) as *const ::core::ffi::c_void,
        n as size_t,
    );
    (*pStat).m_comment[n as usize] = '\0' as i32 as ::core::ffi::c_char;
    (*pStat).m_is_directory = mz_zip_reader_is_file_a_directory(pZip, file_index);
    (*pStat).m_is_encrypted = mz_zip_reader_is_file_encrypted(pZip, file_index);
    (*pStat).m_is_supported = mz_zip_reader_is_file_supported(pZip, file_index);
    if (if (if (*pStat).m_comp_size > (*pStat).m_uncomp_size {
        (*pStat).m_comp_size
    } else {
        (*pStat).m_uncomp_size
    }) > (*pStat).m_local_header_ofs
    {
        (if (*pStat).m_comp_size > (*pStat).m_uncomp_size {
            (*pStat).m_comp_size
        } else {
            (*pStat).m_uncomp_size
        })
    } else {
        (*pStat).m_local_header_ofs
    }) == MZ_UINT32_MAX as mz_uint64
    {
        let mut extra_size_remaining: mz_uint32 =
            *p.offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32
                | (*p
                    .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                    .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
                    << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint;
        if extra_size_remaining != 0 {
            let mut pExtra_data: *const mz_uint8 = p
                .offset(MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                .offset(
                    (*p.offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                        .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32
                        | (*p
                            .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                            .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                            as mz_uint32)
                            << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint) as isize,
                );
            loop {
                let mut field_id: mz_uint32 = 0;
                let mut field_data_size: mz_uint32 = 0;
                if (extra_size_remaining as usize)
                    < (::core::mem::size_of::<mz_uint16>() as usize).wrapping_mul(2 as usize)
                {
                    return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_HEADER_OR_CORRUPTED);
                }
                field_id = *pExtra_data.offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32
                    | (*pExtra_data.offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
                        << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint;
                field_data_size = *pExtra_data
                    .offset(::core::mem::size_of::<mz_uint16>() as usize as isize)
                    .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                    as mz_uint32
                    | (*pExtra_data
                        .offset(::core::mem::size_of::<mz_uint16>() as usize as isize)
                        .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                        as mz_uint32)
                        << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint;
                if (field_data_size as usize).wrapping_add(
                    (::core::mem::size_of::<mz_uint16>() as usize).wrapping_mul(2 as usize),
                ) > extra_size_remaining as usize
                {
                    return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_HEADER_OR_CORRUPTED);
                }
                if field_id
                    == MZ_ZIP64_EXTENDED_INFORMATION_FIELD_HEADER_ID as ::core::ffi::c_int
                        as mz_uint32
                {
                    let mut pField_data: *const mz_uint8 = pExtra_data.offset(
                        (::core::mem::size_of::<mz_uint16>() as usize).wrapping_mul(2 as usize)
                            as isize,
                    );
                    let mut field_data_remaining: mz_uint32 = field_data_size;
                    if !pFound_zip64_extra_data.is_null() {
                        *pFound_zip64_extra_data = MZ_TRUE as mz_bool;
                    }
                    if (*pStat).m_uncomp_size == MZ_UINT32_MAX as mz_uint64 {
                        if (field_data_remaining as usize)
                            < ::core::mem::size_of::<mz_uint64>() as usize
                        {
                            return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_HEADER_OR_CORRUPTED);
                        }
                        (*pStat).m_uncomp_size = (*pField_data
                            .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                            as mz_uint32
                            | (*pField_data.offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
                                << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
                            | (*pField_data.offset(2 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
                                << 16 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
                            | (*pField_data.offset(3 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
                                << 24 as ::core::ffi::c_uint)
                            as mz_uint64
                            | ((*pField_data
                                .offset(::core::mem::size_of::<mz_uint32>() as usize as isize)
                                .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                                as mz_uint32
                                | (*pField_data
                                    .offset(::core::mem::size_of::<mz_uint32>() as usize as isize)
                                    .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                                    as mz_uint32)
                                    << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
                                | (*pField_data
                                    .offset(::core::mem::size_of::<mz_uint32>() as usize as isize)
                                    .offset(2 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                                    as mz_uint32)
                                    << 16 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
                                | (*pField_data
                                    .offset(::core::mem::size_of::<mz_uint32>() as usize as isize)
                                    .offset(3 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                                    as mz_uint32)
                                    << 24 as ::core::ffi::c_uint)
                                as mz_uint64)
                                << 32 as ::core::ffi::c_uint;
                        pField_data = pField_data
                            .offset(::core::mem::size_of::<mz_uint64>() as usize as isize);
                        field_data_remaining = (field_data_remaining as ::core::ffi::c_ulong)
                            .wrapping_sub(::core::mem::size_of::<mz_uint64>() as usize
                                as ::core::ffi::c_ulong)
                            as mz_uint32
                            as mz_uint32;
                    }
                    if (*pStat).m_comp_size == MZ_UINT32_MAX as mz_uint64 {
                        if (field_data_remaining as usize)
                            < ::core::mem::size_of::<mz_uint64>() as usize
                        {
                            return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_HEADER_OR_CORRUPTED);
                        }
                        (*pStat).m_comp_size = (*pField_data
                            .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                            as mz_uint32
                            | (*pField_data.offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
                                << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
                            | (*pField_data.offset(2 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
                                << 16 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
                            | (*pField_data.offset(3 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
                                << 24 as ::core::ffi::c_uint)
                            as mz_uint64
                            | ((*pField_data
                                .offset(::core::mem::size_of::<mz_uint32>() as usize as isize)
                                .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                                as mz_uint32
                                | (*pField_data
                                    .offset(::core::mem::size_of::<mz_uint32>() as usize as isize)
                                    .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                                    as mz_uint32)
                                    << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
                                | (*pField_data
                                    .offset(::core::mem::size_of::<mz_uint32>() as usize as isize)
                                    .offset(2 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                                    as mz_uint32)
                                    << 16 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
                                | (*pField_data
                                    .offset(::core::mem::size_of::<mz_uint32>() as usize as isize)
                                    .offset(3 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                                    as mz_uint32)
                                    << 24 as ::core::ffi::c_uint)
                                as mz_uint64)
                                << 32 as ::core::ffi::c_uint;
                        pField_data = pField_data
                            .offset(::core::mem::size_of::<mz_uint64>() as usize as isize);
                        field_data_remaining = (field_data_remaining as ::core::ffi::c_ulong)
                            .wrapping_sub(::core::mem::size_of::<mz_uint64>() as usize
                                as ::core::ffi::c_ulong)
                            as mz_uint32
                            as mz_uint32;
                    }
                    if (*pStat).m_local_header_ofs == MZ_UINT32_MAX as mz_uint64 {
                        if (field_data_remaining as usize)
                            < ::core::mem::size_of::<mz_uint64>() as usize
                        {
                            return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_HEADER_OR_CORRUPTED);
                        }
                        (*pStat).m_local_header_ofs = (*pField_data
                            .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                            as mz_uint32
                            | (*pField_data.offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
                                << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
                            | (*pField_data.offset(2 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
                                << 16 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
                            | (*pField_data.offset(3 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
                                << 24 as ::core::ffi::c_uint)
                            as mz_uint64
                            | ((*pField_data
                                .offset(::core::mem::size_of::<mz_uint32>() as usize as isize)
                                .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                                as mz_uint32
                                | (*pField_data
                                    .offset(::core::mem::size_of::<mz_uint32>() as usize as isize)
                                    .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                                    as mz_uint32)
                                    << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
                                | (*pField_data
                                    .offset(::core::mem::size_of::<mz_uint32>() as usize as isize)
                                    .offset(2 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                                    as mz_uint32)
                                    << 16 as ::core::ffi::c_uint
                                | (*pField_data
                                    .offset(::core::mem::size_of::<mz_uint32>() as usize as isize)
                                    .offset(3 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                                    as mz_uint32)
                                    << 24 as ::core::ffi::c_uint)
                                as mz_uint64)
                                << 32 as ::core::ffi::c_uint;
                        pField_data = pField_data
                            .offset(::core::mem::size_of::<mz_uint64>() as usize as isize);
                        field_data_remaining = (field_data_remaining as ::core::ffi::c_ulong)
                            .wrapping_sub(::core::mem::size_of::<mz_uint64>() as usize
                                as ::core::ffi::c_ulong)
                            as mz_uint32
                            as mz_uint32;
                    }
                    break;
                } else {
                    pExtra_data = pExtra_data.offset(
                        (::core::mem::size_of::<mz_uint16>() as usize)
                            .wrapping_mul(2 as usize)
                            .wrapping_add(field_data_size as usize)
                            as isize,
                    );
                    extra_size_remaining = (extra_size_remaining as usize)
                        .wrapping_sub(
                            (::core::mem::size_of::<mz_uint16>() as usize).wrapping_mul(2 as usize),
                        )
                        .wrapping_sub(field_data_size as usize)
                        as mz_uint32;
                    if !(extra_size_remaining != 0) {
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    return MZ_TRUE;
}
#[inline(always)]
unsafe extern "C" fn mz_zip_string_equal(
    mut pA: *const ::core::ffi::c_char,
    mut pB: *const ::core::ffi::c_char,
    mut len: mz_uint,
    mut flags: mz_uint,
) -> mz_bool {
    let mut i: mz_uint = 0;
    if flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_CASE_SENSITIVE as ::core::ffi::c_int as mz_uint != 0 {
        return (0 as ::core::ffi::c_int
            == memcmp(
                pA as *const ::core::ffi::c_void,
                pB as *const ::core::ffi::c_void,
                len as size_t,
            )) as ::core::ffi::c_int;
    }
    i = 0 as mz_uint;
    while i < len {
        if (if *pA.offset(i as isize) as ::core::ffi::c_int >= 'A' as i32
            && *pA.offset(i as isize) as ::core::ffi::c_int <= 'Z' as i32
        {
            *pA.offset(i as isize) as ::core::ffi::c_int - 'A' as i32 + 'a' as i32
        } else {
            *pA.offset(i as isize) as ::core::ffi::c_int
        }) != (if *pB.offset(i as isize) as ::core::ffi::c_int >= 'A' as i32
            && *pB.offset(i as isize) as ::core::ffi::c_int <= 'Z' as i32
        {
            *pB.offset(i as isize) as ::core::ffi::c_int - 'A' as i32 + 'a' as i32
        } else {
            *pB.offset(i as isize) as ::core::ffi::c_int
        }) {
            return MZ_FALSE;
        }
        i = i.wrapping_add(1);
    }
    return MZ_TRUE;
}
#[inline(always)]
unsafe extern "C" fn mz_zip_filename_compare(
    mut pCentral_dir_array: *const mz_zip_array,
    mut pCentral_dir_offsets: *const mz_zip_array,
    mut l_index: mz_uint,
    mut pR: *const ::core::ffi::c_char,
    mut r_len: mz_uint,
) -> ::core::ffi::c_int {
    let mut pL: *const mz_uint8 = ((*pCentral_dir_array).m_p as *mut mz_uint8)
        .offset(*((*pCentral_dir_offsets).m_p as *mut mz_uint32).offset(l_index as isize) as isize)
        as *mut mz_uint8;
    let mut pE: *const mz_uint8 = ::core::ptr::null::<mz_uint8>();
    let mut l_len: mz_uint = *pL
        .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
        .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint
        | (*pL
            .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
            .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint)
            << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint;
    let mut l: mz_uint8 = 0 as mz_uint8;
    let mut r: mz_uint8 = 0 as mz_uint8;
    pL = pL.offset(MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize);
    pE = pL.offset((if l_len < r_len { l_len } else { r_len }) as isize);
    while pL < pE {
        l = (if *pL as ::core::ffi::c_int >= 'A' as i32 && *pL as ::core::ffi::c_int <= 'Z' as i32 {
            *pL as ::core::ffi::c_int - 'A' as i32 + 'a' as i32
        } else {
            *pL as ::core::ffi::c_int
        }) as mz_uint8;
        r = (if *pR as ::core::ffi::c_int >= 'A' as i32 && *pR as ::core::ffi::c_int <= 'Z' as i32 {
            *pR as ::core::ffi::c_int - 'A' as i32 + 'a' as i32
        } else {
            *pR as ::core::ffi::c_int
        }) as mz_uint8;
        if l as ::core::ffi::c_int != r as ::core::ffi::c_int {
            break;
        }
        pL = pL.offset(1);
        pR = pR.offset(1);
    }
    return if pL == pE {
        l_len.wrapping_sub(r_len) as ::core::ffi::c_int
    } else {
        l as ::core::ffi::c_int - r as ::core::ffi::c_int
    };
}
unsafe extern "C" fn mz_zip_locate_file_binary_search(
    mut pZip: *mut mz_zip_archive,
    mut pFilename: *const ::core::ffi::c_char,
    mut pIndex: *mut mz_uint32,
) -> mz_bool {
    let mut pState: *mut mz_zip_internal_state = (*pZip).m_pState;
    let mut pCentral_dir_offsets: *const mz_zip_array = &raw mut (*pState).m_central_dir_offsets;
    let mut pCentral_dir: *const mz_zip_array = &raw mut (*pState).m_central_dir;
    let mut pIndices: *mut mz_uint32 =
        ((*pState).m_sorted_central_dir_offsets.m_p as *mut mz_uint32)
            .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as *mut mz_uint32;
    let size: mz_uint32 = (*pZip).m_total_files;
    let filename_len: mz_uint = strlen(pFilename) as mz_uint;
    if !pIndex.is_null() {
        *pIndex = 0 as mz_uint32;
    }
    if size != 0 {
        let mut l: mz_int64 = 0 as mz_int64;
        let mut h: mz_int64 = size as mz_int64 - 1 as mz_int64;
        while l <= h {
            let mut m: mz_int64 = l + (h - l >> 1 as ::core::ffi::c_int);
            let mut file_index: mz_uint32 = *pIndices.offset(m as mz_uint32 as isize);
            let mut comp: ::core::ffi::c_int = mz_zip_filename_compare(
                pCentral_dir,
                pCentral_dir_offsets,
                file_index as mz_uint,
                pFilename,
                filename_len,
            );
            if comp == 0 {
                if !pIndex.is_null() {
                    *pIndex = file_index;
                }
                return MZ_TRUE;
            } else if comp < 0 as ::core::ffi::c_int {
                l = m + 1 as mz_int64;
            } else {
                h = m - 1 as mz_int64;
            }
        }
    }
    return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_FILE_NOT_FOUND);
}
#[no_mangle]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn mz_zip_reader_locate_file(
    mut pZip: *mut mz_zip_archive,
    mut pName: *const ::core::ffi::c_char,
    mut pComment: *const ::core::ffi::c_char,
    mut flags: mz_uint,
) -> ::core::ffi::c_int {
    let mut index: mz_uint32 = 0;
    if mz_zip_reader_locate_file_v2(pZip, pName, pComment, flags, &raw mut index) == 0 {
        return -(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int);
    } else {
        return index as ::core::ffi::c_int;
    };
}
#[no_mangle]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn mz_zip_reader_locate_file_v2(
    mut pZip: *mut mz_zip_archive,
    mut pName: *const ::core::ffi::c_char,
    mut pComment: *const ::core::ffi::c_char,
    mut flags: mz_uint,
    mut pIndex: *mut mz_uint32,
) -> mz_bool {
    let mut file_index: mz_uint = 0;
    let mut name_len: size_t = 0;
    let mut comment_len: size_t = 0;
    if !pIndex.is_null() {
        *pIndex = 0 as mz_uint32;
    }
    if pZip.is_null() || (*pZip).m_pState.is_null() || pName.is_null() {
        return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_PARAMETER);
    }
    if (*(*pZip).m_pState).m_init_flags
        & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_DO_NOT_SORT_CENTRAL_DIRECTORY as ::core::ffi::c_int as mz_uint32
        == 0 as mz_uint32
        && (*pZip).m_zip_mode as ::core::ffi::c_uint
            == MZ_ZIP_MODE_READING as ::core::ffi::c_int as ::core::ffi::c_uint
        && flags
            & (MZ_ZIP_FLAG_IGNORE_PATH as ::core::ffi::c_int
                | MZ_ZIP_FLAG_CASE_SENSITIVE as ::core::ffi::c_int) as mz_uint
            == 0 as mz_uint
        && pComment.is_null()
        && (*(*pZip).m_pState).m_sorted_central_dir_offsets.m_size != 0
    {
        return mz_zip_locate_file_binary_search(pZip, pName, pIndex);
    }
    name_len = strlen(pName);
    if name_len > MZ_UINT16_MAX as size_t {
        return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_PARAMETER);
    }
    comment_len = if !pComment.is_null() {
        strlen(pComment)
    } else {
        0 as size_t
    };
    if comment_len > MZ_UINT16_MAX as size_t {
        return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_PARAMETER);
    }
    let mut current_block_24: u64;
    file_index = 0 as mz_uint;
    while file_index < (*pZip).m_total_files {
        let mut pHeader: *const mz_uint8 = ((*(*pZip).m_pState).m_central_dir.m_p as *mut mz_uint8)
            .offset(
                *((*(*pZip).m_pState).m_central_dir_offsets.m_p as *mut mz_uint32)
                    .offset(file_index as isize) as isize,
            ) as *mut mz_uint8;
        let mut filename_len: mz_uint = *pHeader
            .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
            .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
            as mz_uint
            | (*pHeader
                .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint)
                << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint;
        let mut pFilename: *const ::core::ffi::c_char = (pHeader as *const ::core::ffi::c_char)
            .offset(MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize);
        if !((filename_len as size_t) < name_len) {
            if comment_len != 0 {
                let mut file_extra_len: mz_uint = *pHeader
                    .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                    .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                    as mz_uint
                    | (*pHeader
                        .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                        .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                        as mz_uint)
                        << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint;
                let mut file_comment_len: mz_uint = *pHeader
                    .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMMENT_LEN_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                    .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                    as mz_uint
                    | (*pHeader
                        .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMMENT_LEN_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                        .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                        as mz_uint)
                        << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint;
                let mut pFile_comment: *const ::core::ffi::c_char = pFilename
                    .offset(filename_len as isize)
                    .offset(file_extra_len as isize);
                if file_comment_len as size_t != comment_len
                    || mz_zip_string_equal(pComment, pFile_comment, file_comment_len, flags) == 0
                {
                    current_block_24 = 13109137661213826276;
                } else {
                    current_block_24 = 5143058163439228106;
                }
            } else {
                current_block_24 = 5143058163439228106;
            }
            match current_block_24 {
                13109137661213826276 => {}
                _ => {
                    if flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_IGNORE_PATH as ::core::ffi::c_int as mz_uint != 0
                        && filename_len != 0
                    {
                        let mut ofs: ::core::ffi::c_int =
                            filename_len.wrapping_sub(1 as mz_uint) as ::core::ffi::c_int;
                        while !(*pFilename.offset(ofs as isize) as ::core::ffi::c_int == '/' as i32
                            || *pFilename.offset(ofs as isize) as ::core::ffi::c_int == '\\' as i32
                            || *pFilename.offset(ofs as isize) as ::core::ffi::c_int == ':' as i32)
                        {
                            ofs -= 1;
                            if !(ofs >= 0 as ::core::ffi::c_int) {
                                break;
                            }
                        }
                        ofs += 1;
                        pFilename = pFilename.offset(ofs as isize);
                        filename_len = filename_len.wrapping_sub(ofs as mz_uint);
                    }
                    if filename_len as size_t == name_len
                        && mz_zip_string_equal(pName, pFilename, filename_len, flags) != 0
                    {
                        if !pIndex.is_null() {
                            *pIndex = file_index as mz_uint32;
                        }
                        return MZ_TRUE;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        file_index = file_index.wrapping_add(1);
    }
    return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_FILE_NOT_FOUND);
}
unsafe extern "C" fn mz_zip_reader_extract_to_mem_no_alloc1(
    mut pZip: *mut mz_zip_archive,
    mut file_index: mz_uint,
    mut pBuf: *mut ::core::ffi::c_void,
    mut buf_size: size_t,
    mut flags: mz_uint,
    mut pUser_read_buf: *mut ::core::ffi::c_void,
    mut user_read_buf_size: size_t,
    mut st: *const mz_zip_archive_file_stat,
) -> mz_bool {
    let mut status: ::core::ffi::c_int = TINFL_STATUS_DONE as ::core::ffi::c_int;
    let mut needed_size: mz_uint64 = 0;
    let mut cur_file_ofs: mz_uint64 = 0;
    let mut comp_remaining: mz_uint64 = 0;
    let mut out_buf_ofs: mz_uint64 = 0 as mz_uint64;
    let mut read_buf_size: mz_uint64 = 0;
    let mut read_buf_ofs: mz_uint64 = 0 as mz_uint64;
    let mut read_buf_avail: mz_uint64 = 0;
    let mut file_stat: mz_zip_archive_file_stat = mz_zip_archive_file_stat {
        m_file_index: 0,
        m_central_dir_ofs: 0,
        m_version_made_by: 0,
        m_version_needed: 0,
        m_bit_flag: 0,
        m_method: 0,
        m_crc32: 0,
        m_comp_size: 0,
        m_uncomp_size: 0,
        m_internal_attr: 0,
        m_external_attr: 0,
        m_local_header_ofs: 0,
        m_comment_size: 0,
        m_is_directory: 0,
        m_is_encrypted: 0,
        m_is_supported: 0,
        m_filename: [0; 512],
        m_comment: [0; 512],
        m_time: 0,
    };
    let mut pRead_buf: *mut ::core::ffi::c_void = ::core::ptr::null_mut::<::core::ffi::c_void>();
    let mut local_header_u32: [mz_uint32; 8] = [0; 8];
    let mut pLocal_header: *mut mz_uint8 =
        &raw mut local_header_u32 as *mut mz_uint32 as *mut mz_uint8;
    let mut inflator: tinfl_decompressor = tinfl_decompressor_tag {
        m_state: 0,
        m_num_bits: 0,
        m_zhdr0: 0,
        m_zhdr1: 0,
        m_z_adler32: 0,
        m_final: 0,
        m_type: 0,
        m_check_adler32: 0,
        m_dist: 0,
        m_counter: 0,
        m_num_extra: 0,
        m_table_sizes: [0; 3],
        m_bit_buf: 0,
        m_dist_from_out_buf_start: 0,
        m_look_up: [[0; 1024]; 3],
        m_tree_0: [0; 576],
        m_tree_1: [0; 64],
        m_tree_2: [0; 38],
        m_code_size_0: [0; 288],
        m_code_size_1: [0; 32],
        m_code_size_2: [0; 19],
        m_raw_header: [0; 4],
        m_len_codes: [0; 457],
    };
    if pZip.is_null()
        || (*pZip).m_pState.is_null()
        || buf_size != 0 && pBuf.is_null()
        || user_read_buf_size != 0 && pUser_read_buf.is_null()
        || (*pZip).m_pRead.is_none()
    {
        return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_PARAMETER);
    }
    if !st.is_null() {
        file_stat = *st;
    } else if mz_zip_reader_file_stat(pZip, file_index, &raw mut file_stat) == 0 {
        return MZ_FALSE;
    }
    if file_stat.m_is_directory != 0 || file_stat.m_comp_size == 0 {
        return MZ_TRUE;
    }
    if file_stat.m_bit_flag as ::core::ffi::c_int
        & (MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_IS_ENCRYPTED as ::core::ffi::c_int
            | MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_USES_STRONG_ENCRYPTION as ::core::ffi::c_int
