        m_pRealloc: None,
        m_pAlloc_opaque: ::core::ptr::null_mut::<::core::ffi::c_void>(),
        m_pRead: None,
        m_pWrite: None,
        m_pNeeds_keepalive: None,
        m_pIO_opaque: ::core::ptr::null_mut::<::core::ffi::c_void>(),
        m_pState: ::core::ptr::null_mut::<mz_zip_internal_state>(),
    };
    let mut p: *mut ::core::ffi::c_void = NULL;
    if !pSize.is_null() {
        *pSize = 0 as size_t;
    }
    if pZip_filename.is_null() || pArchive_name.is_null() {
        if !pErr.is_null() {
            *pErr = MZ_ZIP_INVALID_PARAMETER;
        }
        return NULL;
    }
    mz_zip_zero_struct(&raw mut zip_archive);
    if mz_zip_reader_init_file_v2(
        &raw mut zip_archive,
        pZip_filename,
        flags | MZ_ZIP_FLAG_DO_NOT_SORT_CENTRAL_DIRECTORY as ::core::ffi::c_int as mz_uint,
        0 as mz_uint64,
        0 as mz_uint64,
    ) == 0
    {
        if !pErr.is_null() {
            *pErr = zip_archive.m_last_error;
        }
        return NULL;
    }
    if mz_zip_reader_locate_file_v2(
        &raw mut zip_archive,
        pArchive_name,
        pComment,
        flags,
        &raw mut file_index,
    ) != 0
    {
        p = mz_zip_reader_extract_to_heap(
            &raw mut zip_archive,
            file_index as mz_uint,
            pSize,
            flags,
        );
    }
    mz_zip_reader_end_internal(&raw mut zip_archive, (p != NULL) as ::core::ffi::c_int);
    if !pErr.is_null() {
        *pErr = zip_archive.m_last_error;
    }
    return p;
}
#[no_mangle]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn mz_zip_extract_archive_file_to_heap(
    mut pZip_filename: *const ::core::ffi::c_char,
    mut pArchive_name: *const ::core::ffi::c_char,
    mut pSize: *mut size_t,
    mut flags: mz_uint,
) -> *mut ::core::ffi::c_void {
    return mz_zip_extract_archive_file_to_heap_v2(
        pZip_filename,
        pArchive_name,
        ::core::ptr::null::<::core::ffi::c_char>(),
        pSize,
        flags,
        ::core::ptr::null_mut::<mz_zip_error>(),
    );
}
#[no_mangle]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn mz_zip_get_mode(mut pZip: *mut mz_zip_archive) -> mz_zip_mode {
    return (if !pZip.is_null() {
        (*pZip).m_zip_mode as ::core::ffi::c_uint
    } else {
        MZ_ZIP_MODE_INVALID as ::core::ffi::c_int as ::core::ffi::c_uint
    }) as mz_zip_mode;
}
#[no_mangle]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn mz_zip_get_type(mut pZip: *mut mz_zip_archive) -> mz_zip_type {
    return (if !pZip.is_null() {
        (*pZip).m_zip_type as ::core::ffi::c_uint
    } else {
        MZ_ZIP_TYPE_INVALID as ::core::ffi::c_int as ::core::ffi::c_uint
    }) as mz_zip_type;
}
#[no_mangle]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn mz_zip_set_last_error(
    mut pZip: *mut mz_zip_archive,
    mut err_num: mz_zip_error,
) -> mz_zip_error {
    let mut prev_err: mz_zip_error = MZ_ZIP_NO_ERROR;
    if pZip.is_null() {
        return MZ_ZIP_INVALID_PARAMETER;
    }
    prev_err = (*pZip).m_last_error;
    (*pZip).m_last_error = err_num;
    return prev_err;
}
#[no_mangle]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn mz_zip_peek_last_error(mut pZip: *mut mz_zip_archive) -> mz_zip_error {
    if pZip.is_null() {
        return MZ_ZIP_INVALID_PARAMETER;
    }
    return (*pZip).m_last_error;
}
#[no_mangle]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn mz_zip_clear_last_error(mut pZip: *mut mz_zip_archive) -> mz_zip_error {
    return mz_zip_set_last_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_NO_ERROR);
}
#[no_mangle]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn mz_zip_get_last_error(mut pZip: *mut mz_zip_archive) -> mz_zip_error {
    let mut prev_err: mz_zip_error = MZ_ZIP_NO_ERROR;
    if pZip.is_null() {
        return MZ_ZIP_INVALID_PARAMETER;
    }
    prev_err = (*pZip).m_last_error;
    (*pZip).m_last_error = MZ_ZIP_NO_ERROR;
    return prev_err;
}
#[no_mangle]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn mz_zip_get_error_string(
    mut mz_err: mz_zip_error,
) -> *const ::core::ffi::c_char {
    match mz_err as ::core::ffi::c_uint {
        0 => return b"no error\0" as *const u8 as *const ::core::ffi::c_char,
        1 => return b"undefined error\0" as *const u8 as *const ::core::ffi::c_char,
        2 => return b"too many files\0" as *const u8 as *const ::core::ffi::c_char,
        3 => return b"file too large\0" as *const u8 as *const ::core::ffi::c_char,
        4 => return b"unsupported method\0" as *const u8 as *const ::core::ffi::c_char,
        5 => {
            return b"unsupported encryption\0" as *const u8 as *const ::core::ffi::c_char;
        }
        6 => return b"unsupported feature\0" as *const u8 as *const ::core::ffi::c_char,
        7 => {
            return b"failed finding central directory\0" as *const u8
                as *const ::core::ffi::c_char;
        }
        8 => return b"not a ZIP archive\0" as *const u8 as *const ::core::ffi::c_char,
        9 => {
            return b"invalid header or archive is corrupted\0" as *const u8
                as *const ::core::ffi::c_char;
        }
        10 => {
            return b"unsupported multidisk archive\0" as *const u8 as *const ::core::ffi::c_char;
        }
        11 => {
            return b"decompression failed or archive is corrupted\0" as *const u8
                as *const ::core::ffi::c_char;
        }
        12 => return b"compression failed\0" as *const u8 as *const ::core::ffi::c_char,
        13 => {
            return b"unexpected decompressed size\0" as *const u8 as *const ::core::ffi::c_char;
        }
        14 => return b"CRC-32 check failed\0" as *const u8 as *const ::core::ffi::c_char,
        15 => {
            return b"unsupported central directory size\0" as *const u8
                as *const ::core::ffi::c_char;
        }
        16 => return b"allocation failed\0" as *const u8 as *const ::core::ffi::c_char,
        17 => return b"file open failed\0" as *const u8 as *const ::core::ffi::c_char,
        18 => return b"file create failed\0" as *const u8 as *const ::core::ffi::c_char,
        19 => return b"file write failed\0" as *const u8 as *const ::core::ffi::c_char,
        20 => return b"file read failed\0" as *const u8 as *const ::core::ffi::c_char,
        21 => return b"file close failed\0" as *const u8 as *const ::core::ffi::c_char,
        22 => return b"file seek failed\0" as *const u8 as *const ::core::ffi::c_char,
        23 => return b"file stat failed\0" as *const u8 as *const ::core::ffi::c_char,
        24 => return b"invalid parameter\0" as *const u8 as *const ::core::ffi::c_char,
        25 => return b"invalid filename\0" as *const u8 as *const ::core::ffi::c_char,
        26 => return b"buffer too small\0" as *const u8 as *const ::core::ffi::c_char,
        27 => return b"internal error\0" as *const u8 as *const ::core::ffi::c_char,
        28 => return b"file not found\0" as *const u8 as *const ::core::ffi::c_char,
        29 => return b"archive is too large\0" as *const u8 as *const ::core::ffi::c_char,
        30 => return b"validation failed\0" as *const u8 as *const ::core::ffi::c_char,
        31 => {
            return b"write callback failed\0" as *const u8 as *const ::core::ffi::c_char;
        }
        32 => return b"total errors\0" as *const u8 as *const ::core::ffi::c_char,
        _ => {}
    }
    return b"unknown error\0" as *const u8 as *const ::core::ffi::c_char;
}
#[no_mangle]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn mz_zip_is_zip64(mut pZip: *mut mz_zip_archive) -> mz_bool {
    if pZip.is_null() || (*pZip).m_pState.is_null() {
        return MZ_FALSE;
    }
    return (*(*pZip).m_pState).m_zip64;
}
#[no_mangle]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn mz_zip_get_central_dir_size(mut pZip: *mut mz_zip_archive) -> size_t {
    if pZip.is_null() || (*pZip).m_pState.is_null() {
        return 0 as size_t;
    }
    return (*(*pZip).m_pState).m_central_dir.m_size;
}
#[no_mangle]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn mz_zip_reader_get_num_files(mut pZip: *mut mz_zip_archive) -> mz_uint {
    return if !pZip.is_null() {
        (*pZip).m_total_files as mz_uint
    } else {
        0 as mz_uint
    };
}
#[no_mangle]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn mz_zip_get_archive_size(mut pZip: *mut mz_zip_archive) -> mz_uint64 {
    if pZip.is_null() {
        return 0 as mz_uint64;
    }
    return (*pZip).m_archive_size;
}
#[no_mangle]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn mz_zip_get_archive_file_start_offset(
    mut pZip: *mut mz_zip_archive,
) -> mz_uint64 {
    if pZip.is_null() || (*pZip).m_pState.is_null() {
        return 0 as mz_uint64;
    }
    return (*(*pZip).m_pState).m_file_archive_start_ofs;
}
#[no_mangle]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn mz_zip_get_cfile(mut pZip: *mut mz_zip_archive) -> *mut FILE {
    if pZip.is_null() || (*pZip).m_pState.is_null() {
        return ::core::ptr::null_mut::<FILE>();
    }
    return (*(*pZip).m_pState).m_pFile;
}
#[no_mangle]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn mz_zip_read_archive_data(
    mut pZip: *mut mz_zip_archive,
    mut file_ofs: mz_uint64,
    mut pBuf: *mut ::core::ffi::c_void,
    mut n: size_t,
) -> size_t {
    if pZip.is_null() || (*pZip).m_pState.is_null() || pBuf.is_null() || (*pZip).m_pRead.is_none() {
        return mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_PARAMETER) as size_t;
    }
    return (*pZip).m_pRead.expect("non-null function pointer")(
        (*pZip).m_pIO_opaque,
        file_ofs,
        pBuf,
        n,
    );
}
#[no_mangle]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn mz_zip_reader_get_filename(
    mut pZip: *mut mz_zip_archive,
    mut file_index: mz_uint,
    mut pFilename: *mut ::core::ffi::c_char,
    mut filename_buf_size: mz_uint,
) -> mz_uint {
    let mut n: mz_uint = 0;
    let mut p: *const mz_uint8 = mz_zip_get_cdh(pZip, file_index);
    if p.is_null() {
        if filename_buf_size != 0 {
            *pFilename.offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) =
                '\0' as i32 as ::core::ffi::c_char;
        }
        mz_zip_set_error(pZip, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_PARAMETER);
        return 0 as mz_uint;
    }
    n = (*p
        .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
        .offset(0 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32
        | (*p
            .offset(MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
            .offset(1 as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize) as mz_uint32)
            << 8 as ::core::ffi::c_uint) as mz_uint;
    if filename_buf_size != 0 {
        n = if n < filename_buf_size.wrapping_sub(1 as mz_uint) {
            n
        } else {
            filename_buf_size.wrapping_sub(1 as mz_uint)
        };
        memcpy(
            pFilename as *mut ::core::ffi::c_void,
            p.offset(MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as ::core::ffi::c_int as isize)
                as *const ::core::ffi::c_void,
            n as size_t,
        );
        *pFilename.offset(n as isize) = '\0' as i32 as ::core::ffi::c_char;
    }
    return n.wrapping_add(1 as mz_uint);
}
#[no_mangle]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn mz_zip_reader_file_stat(
    mut pZip: *mut mz_zip_archive,
    mut file_index: mz_uint,
    mut pStat: *mut mz_zip_archive_file_stat,
) -> mz_bool {
    return mz_zip_file_stat_internal(
        pZip,
        file_index,
        mz_zip_get_cdh(pZip, file_index),
        pStat,
        ::core::ptr::null_mut::<mz_bool>(),
    );
}
#[no_mangle]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn mz_zip_end(mut pZip: *mut mz_zip_archive) -> mz_bool {
    if pZip.is_null() {
        return MZ_FALSE;
    }
    if (*pZip).m_zip_mode as ::core::ffi::c_uint
        == MZ_ZIP_MODE_READING as ::core::ffi::c_int as ::core::ffi::c_uint
    {
        return mz_zip_reader_end(pZip);
    } else if (*pZip).m_zip_mode as ::core::ffi::c_uint
        == MZ_ZIP_MODE_WRITING as ::core::ffi::c_int as ::core::ffi::c_uint
        || (*pZip).m_zip_mode as ::core::ffi::c_uint
            == MZ_ZIP_MODE_WRITING_HAS_BEEN_FINALIZED as ::core::ffi::c_int as ::core::ffi::c_uint
    {
        return mz_zip_writer_end(pZip);
    }
    return MZ_FALSE;
}
